Don jingsngewphylla ban khot ia kane ka 
KOT (BOOK) da ka ktien TAPPLI ? 

Kaei kaba kham kit jingmut: ka KOT ne ka “Tappli”? 

1. Ka Tap — Ka mut ka shisong ki sla tympew 

ba la tehsong lang. 

2. Ka Tappli — Na ka Ktien, “Tap” ne “Shi Tap” 

— Ka mut ka shisong ki sla ba* 
lathoh, bad lasuhpyndait lang na 
ka bynta ban pule. Ka long kata 
kaba ngi lah ban tap, bad ban plie 
bad ban pule.

Ki Nongpule, 

Nongpule badon burom, nga ladep thoh ia kane 
ka kot naduh ka snem 1973. Hynrei ngam shym 
lalah ban pynmih bar ia ka, tad haduh mynta, 
naba ngam shym lalah ban phah shon na ka daw 
ba ngam don bai-siew fsbai ban sicw) ban ai ia

7 snem kynthih ban ioh shon bad pynmih tang

ka jingbyra don sbai ne jingbyradon spah ka lah 
ban pynwit bad pynslcm ia u briew ba duk ban

KI TIEN - LAMPHRANG. 

Ha Ka Jingshisha Ngi la Kynduh Lang 


Ki bricw kiba wad ia ka jingshisha baroh ki ia 
kynduh lang ha ka jingshisha wat la kina shym ladon 
ka jingialeit iawan bad ka jingia ai bad jingiashim 
iingmut kylliang ha kaba wad ia ka. Te haba iashem 
iang ha ka juh ka lynti jong ka jingshisha hangta ka 
don ka jinqkmen, ka don ka jingieit bad ka jingialong 
kawci. Hynrei don ki bricw kiba sngewrem ban 
shem ba ki iaid ha ka juh ka lynti ba kiwei ki laiaid. 
Kumta ki pyrshang ban thaaw da kawei pat ka lynti 
ialade tang ban pyniapher na kiwei Kane ka long 
ka bama, naba kam long kaba bha ia uwei ban iaid 
na ktieh tang ban pyniapher n i uta uwei pat uba 
la-iaid ha ka lynti babha Don pat ruh kiba kana 
jong ia ka jingshisha tang naba ki la ioh ban iaid 
shuwa ha ka. Kumta ki niew ia kiwei pat kiba iaid 
na ka juh ka lynti hadien jongki ba ki long tang ki

nongshim kylliang. Ka jingshisha ka long kajingaiei 
na U Blei. Ka long solonsar ia baroh. Uta uba wad 
u shem iaka. Bad uta uba shem ia 1 ^ dei pat ban 
pynpaw ha ki wei - urn dei aril ban bat ia ka tang 
ha lade hi. Ka jingshisha ka ialam sha ka jingshisha. 

Naduh ki sngi mynbarim bad haduh kine ki sngi 
ba mynta don boon byllien ki jait-bynriew bapher ha 
kane ka Pyrthei. Napdeng jong kine don bttn byllien 
kiba la-iaid shaphrang ha ka jingnang jingstad bad ki 
shong ki sah ha ki nongbah. Napdeng kine ki don 
boon byllien ruh kiba dang sah dien ha ka jingnang 
jingstad ba ki shong ki sah ha ki nongrit bad ha ki Ti 
khlaaw. Kine ki jaitbynriew bapher ki don la ki 
ktien ki bapher, bad ki don ruh b ki rukom im ki 
bapher. Bad ki ia don ruh ka jingiapher ha ki dur 
ki dar bad ha ki rup ki rong jong ki snieh doh jongki. 
Hynrei baroh ki don kawei ka doh ka snaam - ka doh 
ka snaam briew. Bad baroh ki don ka juh ka jinglong 
tynraai kaba wad ban ia im bad iashong iasah lang ha 
ka kynhoon kum ka jait bynriew. Ki iawad ruh ia ki

bynriew wat lida ki ia shong jngaai na kawei ka dong

Ban pynsuk ia ka iaid ka ieng na kawei ka liang

bad ban thaaw lad kumno ban leh khnang ba u lah 
ban jaam sha shiliang. Te na kane ka jingpyrkhat

KA JINGPYN1AKHLAD IA U BRIEW

U Dieng sohpet Bneng u long u Dieng jong 
ka Hok ka Riti. Ka jingpyniakhlad noh bak ia 
ka Pyrthei na ka Bneng, ka jingpyniakhlad noh

long ka jingpynshitom ia u briew namar kata ka 
jingpynkhein riti. Ngim lah ban mut dur haduh 
katno ka jingkhia jong kata ka jingpynshitom.

ka long ka jingpynshitom kaba khia haduh kattu

u briew u la pynkhein riti. Kumta ba ha ka jaka 
jong ka Jingshai, ka jingdum tliw ka la wan tap ha 
ka Pyrthei baroh kawei.

UEI U DIENGIEI ? 

Ki Khasi haduh mynta ki ngeit ba u Diengiei 
u la don da shisha. Haduh kine ki sngi, habajia 
ba ki lap ia u tnat diengbah, ne ia ki thied dieng-

jingtap jong ka Khyndew, ki Khas> ki ia ong,

u Diengiei.” 

11 Lum Diengiei u dang don haduh mynta. Une 
u Lum u don shaphang sepngi na ka Sor Shillong 
ka ba long ka Nongbah jong ka Ri Khasi. Urn 
shym jngai na kane ka Sor bad lah ban iohi ia u 
kyndiang na u Lum Shillong. U Lum Diengiei 
u long ruh shaphang sepngi na u Lum-sohpet Bneng. 
Kine ki Lum baroh ar ki ia jngai u wei na uwei pat. 
Hynrei haba peit na u Lum Shillong lah ban 
iohi kyndiang ia ki baroh artylli. U Lum Diengiei 
u dang don haduh mynta. Hynrei u Diengiei te urn 
don shuh naduh ba la pynkyllon ia u. 

U Diengiei uba la pyiar la ki tnat sha suin 
Bneng kat haduh ban da taplup ia ka Pyrthei, bad 
kah dum ngain ia ka Pyrthei baroh kawei na ka 
jingshai jong ka Sngi urn long u dieng uba shisha.
